```python
import time
import math
import numpy as np


def project_density(x, target):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).copy()
    n = x.size
    if n == 0:
        return x
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = float(target)
    s = float(np.sum(x))
    if abs(s - target) <= 1e-12 * max(1, n):
        return x

    lo = -1.0
    hi = 1.0
    for _ in range(80):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.sum(np.clip(x + mid, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if s < target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid

    h = np.clip(x + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-11:
        free = np.flatnonzero((h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12))
        if free.size > 0:
            h[free] += diff / float(free.size)
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    diff2 = target - float(np.sum(h))
    for _ in range(4):
        if abs(diff2) <= 1e-12:
            break
        if diff2 > 0:
            pos = np.flatnonzero(h < 1.0 - 1e-12)
        else:
            pos = np.flatnonzero(h > 1e-12)
        if pos.size == 0:
            break
        if diff2 > 0:
            idx = int(pos[int(np.argmax(1.0 - h[pos]))])
        else:
            idx = int(pos[int(np.argmax(h[pos]))])
        h[idx] += diff2
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
        diff2 = target - float(np.sum(h))
    return h


def corr_array(h):
    return np.correlate(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), 1.0 - np.asarray(h, dtype=float), mode="full")


def eval_c5(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = corr_array(h)
    return float(np.max(a) * (2.0 / float(n)))


def upsample_h(parent, n):
    p = np.asarray(parent, dtype=float)
    n0 = p.size
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=float)
    if n0 == n:
        return project_density(p, 0.5 * float(n))
    if n0 <= 0:
        return project_density(np.full(n, 0.5), 0.5 * float(n))
    x0 = (np.arange(n0, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n0))
    x1 = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
    h = np.interp(x1, x0, p, left=float(p[0]), right=float(p[-1]))
    return project_density(h, 0.5 * float(n))


def binarize_h(parent, n):
    h = upsample_h(parent, n)
    n = int(h.size)
    b = (h > 0.5).astype(float)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    diff = target - float(np.sum(b))
    k = int(round(diff))
    if k > 0:
        z = np.flatnonzero(b < 0.5)
        if z.size > 0:
            order = np.argsort(h[z])[::-1]
            flip = order[:min(k, z.size)]
            b[flip] = 1.0
    elif k < 0:
        o = np.flatnonzero(b > 0.5)
        if o.size > 0:
            order = np.argsort(h[o])
            flip = order[:min(-k, o.size)]
            b[flip] = 0.0
    return project_density(b, target)


def binary_runs_start(n, rng, min_runs=10, max_runs=50):
    n = int(n)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    if n < 4:
        return project_density(np.full(n, 0.5), target)

    min_runs = max(2, min(min_runs, max(2, n // 2)))
    max_runs = max(min_runs, min(max_runs, n))
    r = int(rng.integers(min_runs, max_runs + 1))
    start_bit = int(rng.integers(2))
    w = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(r, dtype=float))
    lengths = np.maximum(np.rint(w * float(n)).astype(int), 1)
    diff = n - int(np.sum(lengths))
    guard = 0
    while diff != 0 and guard < 10000:
        if diff > 0:
            lengths[int(rng.integers(r))] += 1
            diff -= 1
        else:
            cand = np.flatnonzero(lengths > 1)
            if cand.size == 0:
                break
            lengths[int(cand[int(rng.integers(cand.size))])] -= 1
            diff += 1
        guard += 1

    parts = []
    bit = float(start_bit)
    for L in lengths:
        L = int(L)
        if L > 0:
            parts.append(np.full(L, bit, dtype=float))
        bit = 1.0 - bit
    if parts:
        arr = np.concatenate(parts)
    else:
        arr = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

    if arr.size < n:
        arr = np.concatenate((arr, np.full(n - arr.size, bit, dtype=float)))
    elif arr.size > n:
        arr = arr[:n]

    cnt = float(np.sum(arr))
    diff = target - cnt
    k = int(round(diff))
    if k > 0:
        z = np.flatnonzero(arr < 0.5)
        if z.size > 0:
            if z.size > k:
                choose = rng.choice(z, size=k, replace=False)
            else:
                choose = z
            arr[choose] = 1.0
    elif k < 0:
        o = np.flatnonzero(arr > 0.5)
        if o.size > 0:
            kk = -k
            if o.size > kk:
                choose = rng.choice(o, size=kk, replace=False)
            else:
                choose = o
            arr[choose] = 0.0

    return project_density(arr, target)


def weyl_start(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    alpha = 0.6180339887498949 + float(rng.uniform(-1e-8, 1e-8))
    shift = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0))
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) * alpha + shift) % 1.0
    arr = (x < 0.5).astype(float)
    return project_density(arr, target)


def sinus_start(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
    f1 = int(rng.integers(2, 7))
    f2 = int(rng.integers(7, 15))
    p1 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
    p2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
    a1 = float(rng.uniform(0.25, 0.45))
    a2 = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.18))
    h = 0.5 + a1 * np.sin(math.pi * f1 * x + p1) + a2 * np.sin(math.pi * f2 * x + p2)
    return project_density(h, target)


def random_sparse_dir(n, rng, max_nonzeros=4):
    n = int(n)
    if n < 2:
        return None
    m = int(min(max_nonzeros, n))
    if m < 2:
        return None
    idx = rng.choice(n, size=m, replace=False)
    vals = rng.normal(size=m)
    vals -= float(np.mean(vals))
    if float(np.linalg.norm(vals)) < 1e-12:
        return None
    d = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    d[idx] = vals
    d -= float(np.mean(d))
    norm = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
    if norm < 1e-12:
        return None
    return d / norm


def make_t_samples(tmax, n_log=44, n_lin=32):
    tmax = float(tmax)
    if tmax <= 1e-14:
        return np.array([0.0], dtype=float)
    parts = [np.array([0.0], dtype=float)]
    if tmax > 1e-5:
        start = max(1e-12, tmax * 1e-12)
        parts.append(np.geomspace(start, tmax, max(2, int(n_log))))
    parts.append(np.linspace(0.0, tmax, max(2, int(n_lin))))
    fixed = (
        1e-10, 1e-9, 1e-8, 1e-7, 1e-6, 1e-5, 1e-4, 5e-4,
        1e-3, 3e-3, 1e-2, 3e-2, 1e-1, 3e-1,
        1.0, 3.0, 10.0, 30.0, 50.0
    )
    fx = [x for x in fixed if 0.0 < x <= tmax]
    if fx:
        parts.append(np.array(fx, dtype=float))
    arr = np.concatenate(parts)
    arr = np.unique(arr)
    return arr


def line_values(A, B, D, T):
    T = np.asarray(T, dtype=float)
    K = T.size
    L = A.size
    vals = np.empty(K, dtype=float)
    if K == 0 or L == 0:
        return vals
    chunk = 128
    for s in range(0, K, chunk):
        e = min(s + chunk, K)
        Ts = T[s:e]
        M = A[:, None] + (B[:, None] * Ts[None, :])
        M -= (D[:, None] * (Ts[None, :] * Ts[None, :]))
        vals[s:e] = np.max(M, axis=0)
    return vals


def line_search_dir(h, A, d, min_improve=1e-10, scan_log=44, scan_lin=32, refine=140):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    A = np.asarray(A, dtype=float)
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=float)
    n = int(h.size)
    L = int(A.size)
    if d.size != n or L != (2 * n - 1):
        return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(d)) or not np.all(np.isfinite(A)):
        return None

    nz_mask = np.abs(d) > 1e-14
    if not np.any(nz_mask):
        return None

    center = n - 1
    pos = d > 1e-14
    if np.any(pos):
        t_up = float(np.min((1.0 - h[pos]) / d[pos]))
    else:
        t_up = 0.0
    neg = d < -1e-14
    if np.any(neg):
        t_down = float(np.min(h[neg] / (-d[neg])))
    else:
        t_down = 0.0
    if t_up < 0.0:
        t_up = 0.0
    if t_down < 0.0:
        t_down = 0.0
    t_up = min(t_up, 50.0) * (1.0 - 1e-12)
    t_down = min(t_down, 50.0) * (1.0 - 1e-12)
    if t_up <= 1e-14 and t_down <= 1e-14:
        return None

    B = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    D = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    nz = np.flatnonzero(nz_mask)
    if nz.size <= 8:
        one_minus_h = 1.0 - h
        rev_1mh = one_minus_h[::-1]
        for p in nz:
            pp = int(p)
            a = float(d[pp])
            if a == 0.0:
                continue
            B[pp:pp + n] += a * rev_1mh
            idx0 = center - pp
            B[idx0:idx0 + pp + 1] -= a * h[:pp + 1]
        for pp in nz:
            ip = int(pp)
            ap = float(d[ip])
            for qq in nz:
                iq = int(qq)
                aq = float(d[iq])
                idx = center + ip - iq
                if 0 <= idx < L:
                    D[idx] += ap * aq
    else:
        one_minus_h = 1.0 - h
        B = np.correlate(d, one_minus_h, mode="full")
        B -= np.correlate(h, d, mode="full")
        D = np.correlate(d, d, mode="full")

    if not (np.all(np.isfinite(B)) and np.all(np.isfinite(D))):
        return None

    S0 = float(np.max(A))
    best_t = 0.0
    best_val = S0

    pos_samples = make_t_samples(t_up, scan_log, scan_lin)
    neg_samples = -make_t_samples(t_down, scan_log, scan_lin)
    T = np.concatenate((pos_samples, neg_samples))
    T = np.unique(T)
    T = T[(T >= -t_down - 1e-12) & (T <= t_up + 1e-12)]
    if T.size > 0:
        vals = line_values(A, B, D, T)
        j = int(np.argmin(vals))
        best_t = float(T[j])
        best_val = float(vals[j])

    if best_val < S0:
        total = max(t_up, t_down, 1e-8)
        span = max(total / 200.0, 1e-6)
        if best_t != 0.0:
            span = max(span, abs(best_t) / 20.0)
        low = max(-t_down, best_t - span)
        high = min(t_up, best_t + span)
        if high > low:
            T2 = np.linspace(low, high, max(16, int(refine)))
            vals2 = line_values(A, B, D, T2)
            j2 = int(np.argmin(vals2))
            t2 = float(T2[j2])
            v2 = float(vals2[j2])
            if v2 < best_val:
                best_t = t2
                best_val = v2

    if abs(best_t) > 1e-14 and best_val < S0 - min_improve:
        A_new = A + B * best_t - D * (best_t * best_t)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(A_new)):
            return None
        best_val = float(np.max(A_new))
        if best_val < S0 - min_improve:
            return (best_t, best_val, A_new)
    return None


def project_simplex(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float)
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    x = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(x)
    u = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=float)
    cond = x > (cssv - 1.0) / u
    rho = np.nonzero(cond)[0]
    if rho.size > 0:
        r = int(rho[-1])
        theta = (cssv[r] - 1.0) / float(r + 1)
    else:
        theta = float(np.max(v)) - 1.0
    w = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    sw = float(np.sum(w))
    if sw <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        w[int(np.argmax(v))] = 1.0
    else:
        w = w / sw
    return w


def select_top_idx(A, K):
    A = np.asarray(A, dtype=float)
    L = A.size
    K = int(K)
    if K <= 0 or L == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=int)
    if K >= L:
        return np.arange(L, dtype=int)
    return np.argpartition(A, L - K)[-K:].astype(int)


def fill_gradient(h, idx, center, g, weight=1.0):
    n = int(h.size)
    q = int(idx) - int(center)
    L = abs(q)
    if L == 0:
        g += weight * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
    else:
        if q < 0:
            g[:n - L] += weight * (1.0 - h[L:])
            g[L:] -= weight * h[:n - L]
        else:
            g[L:] += weight * (1.0 - h[:n - L])
            g[:n - L] -= weight * h[L:]


def min_norm_gradient(h, A, K, iters=24):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    A = np.asarray(A, dtype=float)
    n = int(h.size)
    center = n - 1
    idx = select_top_idx(A, K)
    K = idx.size
    if K == 0:
        return None
    G = np.empty((K, n), dtype=float)
    for r in range(K):
        G[r, :] = 0.0
        fill_gradient(h, int(idx[r]), center, G[r], 1.0)
    if K == 1:
        return G[0].copy()

    w = np.full(K, 1.0 / float(K), dtype=float)
    for _ in range(max(1, int(iters))):
        GW = G.dot(w)
        grad = 2.0 * G.dot(GW)
        gn = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if gn < 1e-14:
            break
        w = w - (0.25 / gn) * grad
        w = project_simplex(w)
    return G.dot(w)


def direction_gradient(h, A, setting):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    A = np.asarray(A, dtype=float)
    mode = int(setting[0])
    K = int(setting[1])
    beta = float(setting[2])
    n = int(h.size)
    if K <= 0 or n == 0:
        return None

    if mode == 1:
        g = min_norm_gradient(h, A, K, iters=24)
        if g is not None and float(np.linalg.norm(g)) > 1e-13:
            return g

    idx = select_top_idx(A, K)
    if idx.size == 0:
        return None
    vals = A[idx]
    S = float(np.max(vals))
    if beta <= 0.0:
        w = np.full(idx.size, 1.0 / float(idx.size), dtype=float)
    else:
        y = beta * (vals - S)
        y = np.clip(y, -50.0, 0.0)
        w = np.exp(y)
        ss = float(np.sum(w))
        if ss <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ss):
            w = np.full(idx.size, 1.0 / float(idx.size), dtype=float)
        else:
            w = w / ss

    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    center = n - 1
    for i, p in zip(idx, w):
        if p > 1e-12:
            fill_gradient(h, int(i), center, g, float(p))
    return g


def top_pair_dirs(n, g, top=4, top_pairs=8):
    n = int(n)
    if g is None or n < 2:
        return []
    g = np.asarray(g, dtype=float)
    top = max(1, int(top))
    pos = np.argsort(-g)[:top]
    neg = np.argsort(g)[:top]
    pairs = []
    for ii in neg:
        ii = int(ii)
        for jj in pos:
            jj = int(jj)
            if ii == jj:
                continue
            score = float(g[jj] - g[ii])
            if score > 1e-12:
                pairs.append((score, ii, jj))
    if not pairs:
        return []
    pairs.sort(reverse=True)
    inv = 1.0 / math.sqrt(2.0)
    dirs = []
    for score, ii, jj in pairs[:max(1, int(top_pairs))]:
        d = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        d[ii] = inv
        d[jj] = -inv
        dirs.append(d)
    return dirs


def apply_line_move(h, A, d, t, A_new, target):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    raw = h + t * d
    h_new = np.clip(raw, 0.0, 1.0)
    need_recompute = (
        not np.all(np.isfinite(h_new)) or
        abs(float(np.sum(raw)) - float(target)) > 1e-8
    )
    if not need_recompute:
        h_new = np.clip(raw, 0.0, 1.0)
        dev = float(np.max(np.abs(raw - h_new))) if h_new.size else 0.0
        if dev > 1e-9:
            need_recompute = True
    if need_recompute:
        h_new = project_density(raw, target)
        A = corr_array(h_new)
        S = float(np.max(A))
        return h_new, A, S, True
    S = float(np.max(A_new))
    return h_new, A_new, S, False


def local_improve(h0, deadline, seed=0):
    h0 = np.asarray(h0, dtype=float)
    n = int(h0.size)
    if n <= 0:
        h0 = np.array([0.5, 0.5], dtype=float)
        n = 2
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)

    h = project_density(h0, target)
    A = corr_array(h)
    S = float(np.max(A))
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_S = S

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    settings = (
        (0, 1, 0.0),
        (0, 3, 30.0),
        (0, 6, 8.0),
        (0, 12, 3.0),
        (1, 8, 0.0),
        (0, 24, 1.5),
        (1, 14, 0.0),
        (0, 40, 0.8),
    )
    mode_i = 0
    stall = 0
    since_recompute = 0
    kick_allow = max(1e-6, 1.5e-4 / dx)

    while time.perf_counter() < float(deadline) - 0.02:
        if S < best_S - 1e-12:
            A_chk = corr_array(h)
            S_chk = float(np.max(A_chk))
            if S_chk < best_S - 1e-12:
                best_S = S_chk
                best_h = h.copy()
                A = A_chk
                S = S_chk
                since_recompute = 0
            else:
                A = A_chk
                S = S_chk

        if S > best_S + 2.0 * kick_allow:
            h = best_h.copy()
            A = corr_array(h)
            S = float(np.max(A))
            stall = 0
            since_recompute = 0

        st = settings[mode_i % len(settings)]
        mode_i += 1
        g = direction_gradient(h, A, st)
        accepted = False

        if g is not None:
            d = -g
            d -= float(np.mean(d))
            if np.all(np.isfinite(d)):
                norm = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
                if norm > 1e-12:
                    d = d / norm
                    res = line_search_dir(h, A, d, 1e-10, 44, 34, 140)
                    if res is not None:
                        t, val, A_new = res
                        h, A, S, recomputed = apply_line_move(h, A, d, t, A_new, target)
                        since_recompute = 0 if recomputed else (since_recompute + 1)
                        accepted = True

        if not accepted and g is not None:
            pdirs = top_pair_dirs(n, g, top=4, top_pairs=8)
            for pd in pdirs:
                res = line_search_dir(h, A, pd, 1e-10, 50, 36, 120)
                if res is not None:
                    t, val, A_new = res
                    h, A, S, recomputed = apply_line_move(h, A, pd, t, A_new, target)
                    since_recompute = 0 if recomputed else (since_recompute + 1)
                    accepted = True
                    break

        if not accepted:
            for _r in range(4):
                d = random_sparse_dir(n, rng, 4)
                if d is None:
                    continue
                res = line_search_dir(h, A, d, 1e-10, 36, 28, 100)
                if res is not None:
                    t, val, A_new = res
                    h, A, S, recomputed = apply_line_move(h, A, d, t, A_new, target)
                    since_recompute = 0 if recomputed else (since_recompute + 1)
                    accepted = True
                    break

        if accepted:
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 40:
                d = random_sparse_dir(n, rng, 5)
                if d is not None:
                    kicked = False
                    for sc in (0.01, 0.03, 0.08, 0.2, 0.5):
                        h_try = project_density(best_h + sc * d, target)
                        A_try = corr_array(h_try)
                        S_try = float(np.max(A_try))
                        if S_try < best_S + kick_allow and S_try < S + kick_allow:
                            h = h_try
                            A = A_try
                            S = S_try
                            since_recompute = 0
                            stall = 15
                            kicked = True
                            break
                    del kicked

        if since_recompute > 35:
            A = corr_array(h)
            S = float(np.max(A))
            since_recompute = 0

    A = corr_array(h)
    S = float(np.max(A))
    if S < best_S:
        best_h = h.copy()
        best_S = S

    final_h = project_density(best_h, target)
    A = corr_array(final_h)
    S = float(np.max(A))
    return final_h, float(S * dx), int(n)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start = time.perf_counter()
    budget = float(budget_s) if budget_s is not None else 1300.0
    deadline = start + max(5.0, budget - 15.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent = None
    try:
        raw = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        if raw.ndim == 1 and raw.size >= 2:
            parent = np.nan_to_num(raw, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0).astype(float, copy=True)
            parent = np.clip(parent, 0.0, 1.0)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        n0 = int(parent.size)
        hbest = project_density(parent, 0.5 * float(n0))
        cbest = eval_c5(hbest)
    else:
        n0 = 600
        hbest = binary_runs_start(n0, rng, 12, 40)
        cbest = eval_c5(hbest)

    base = max(60, int(hbest.size))
    cands = set()
    for mult in (1, 2, 3, 4):
        n = int(round(base * float(mult)))
        if n % 2:
            n += 1
        if 40 <= n <= 2250:
            cands.add(n)
    for extra in (900, 1800):
        if 40 <= extra <= 2250:
            cands.add(extra)
    n_list = sorted(cands)

    if len(n_list) > 5:
        wanted = []
        for target_n in (base, 2 * base, max(base, n_list[-2]), n_list[-1]):
            best_n = None
            best_diff = 10 ** 9
            for xx in n_list:
                diff = abs(xx - target_n)
                if diff < best_diff:
                    best_diff = diff
                    best_n = xx
            if best_n is not None and best_n not in wanted:
                wanted.append(best_n)
        n_list = sorted(wanted[:5])

    weights = []
    for n in n_list:
        if n <= 1.3 * base:
            weights.append(1.0)
        elif n <= 2.5 * base:
            weights.append(2.5)
        else:
            weights.append(2.0)
    total_w = float(sum(weights))
    total_time = max(0.0, deadline - time.perf_counter() - 5.0)
    time_alloc = []
    for w in weights:
        time_alloc.append(max(15.0, total_time * (float(w) / total_w)))

    for idx_n in range(len(n_list)):
        ni = int(n_list[idx_n])
        rem = deadline - time.perf_counter()
        if rem < 18.0:
            break
        res_alloc = min(time_alloc[idx_n], max(0.0, rem - 4.0))
        if res_alloc <= 10.0:
            break

        hstart = upsample_h(hbest, ni)
        sub = min(res_alloc * 0.50, deadline - time.perf_counter() - 2.0)
        if sub > 8.0:
            h_out, c_out, _ = local_improve(hstart, time.perf_counter() + sub, seed + 101)
            if c_out < cbest:
                hbest = h_out
                cbest = c_out

        if time.perf_counter() < deadline - 12.0:
            hstart = binarize_h(hbest, ni)
            sub = min(res_alloc * 0.25, deadline - time.perf_counter() - 2.0)
            if sub > 8.0:
                h_out, c_out, _ = local_improve(hstart, time.perf_counter() + sub, seed + 202)
                if c_out < cbest:
                    hbest = h_out
                    cbest = c_out

        if time.perf_counter() < deadline - 10.0:
            hstart = binary_runs_start(ni, rng, 12, 45)
            sub = min(res_alloc * 0.15, deadline - time.perf_counter() - 2.0)
            if sub > 8.0:
                h_out, c_out, _ = local_improve(hstart, time.perf_counter() + sub, seed + 303)
                if c_out < cbest:
                    hbest = h_out
                    cbest = c_out

        if time.perf_counter() < deadline - 8.0:
            hstart = weyl_start(ni, rng)
            sub = min(res_alloc * 0.10, deadline - time.perf_counter() - 2.0)
            if sub > 8.0:
                h_out, c_out, _ = local_improve(hstart, time.perf_counter() + sub, seed + 404)
                if c_out < cbest:
                    hbest = h_out
                    cbest = c_out

        if time.perf_counter() < deadline - 7.0:
            hstart = sinus_start(ni, rng)
            sub = min(res_alloc * 0.10, deadline - time.perf_counter() - 2.0)
            if sub > 8.0:
                h_out, c_out, _ = local_improve(hstart, time.perf_counter() + sub, seed + 505)
                if c_out < cbest:
                    hbest = h_out
                    cbest = c_out

    rem = deadline - time.perf_counter()
    if hbest is not None and rem > 12.0:
        h_out, c_out, _ = local_improve(hbest, deadline - 1.0, seed + 9999)
        if c_out < cbest:
            hbest = h_out
            cbest = c_out

    final_h = project_density(hbest, 0.5 * float(hbest.size))
    final_c = eval_c5(final_h)
    return final_h, float(final_c), int(final_h.size)
```
